*page 1*




Adnan a tsaye yake a gaban mudubi yana gyara zaman hular kansa sanye yake da Ash d'in shadda mai bak'in surfani shaddar ta dace da kalar fatarsa kasancewarsa baki mai d'an kyau 

Agogon hannunsa yake sawa Aliya ta fito daga band'akin tana gyara zanin jikinta sanye take da wani bakin less mai torches d'in pink ya bala'in amsarta kasancewarta fara kyakyawa ce ba laifi Dan baza'a sata sahun munana ba.


Wardrobe d'inta ta bud'e ta d'auko wani mayafi pink Wanda ya dace da kalar kayanta ta fara k'ok'arin yafawa akanta 

Adnan da tunda ta fito daga band'aki yake binta da kallo ganin ta d'auko k'aton mayafi zata yafa akanta ne yasa ya juyo yana ajiye turaren dayake fesawa yace 

"Ke mai haka"?

Aliya da mamaki tace " kamarya fa" ta cigaba da gyara zaman mayafin akanta

"Ba  dai haka kike tunanin zaki bini dinner abokina ba"?

A hankali Aliya ta ajiye hannunta jiki a sanyaye tace "mai aibun shigar danayi"?


Tsaki adnan yayi yace "u must be joking idan kina tunanin ahaka zanje wajen dinner abokina dake gwara ki sha zamanki Dan babu yanda za'ayi abokaina suga matata ahaka sai kiyi ta Abu kamar ba wayayya ba ji wani katon mayafin da kika d'auko kamar gidan mutuwa zaki haba Aliya wai meyesa kike hakane yaushe zaki waye ki daina zama cikin duhu gaskiya kina kwapsa min Aliya Dan Allah ki dubi neighbour d'inki wayayya 'yar gayu bata kai ki kyau ba amma ta bala'in iya dressing ke kuwa kamar 'yar kauye wlh 
gaskiya ki canja kayan jikinki completely ki samu skirt haka ko fitted gown ki samu medium size Vail ki yafa mu tafi amma kam idan dai bazaki canza shigar nan ba ki sha zamanki gaskiya"


Aliya a hankali ta cire mayafin jikinta ta koma kan gado ta zauna ta buga tagumi tana kallon Adnan daya d'auko well polished cover shoe d'insa yana sawa yana 'yan wake wakensa a k'asan zuciyarta tana mishi add'uar shiriya Dan idan yana wani Abun kamar wanda bashi da addini


Adnan k'ara juyowa yayi yana kallonta yace " au da ki canza shigar jikinki gwara ki hakura da zuwa kenan"?


Rai a d'an b'ace Aliya tace "bazan canja shigar jikina ba indai sai na canza zaka tafi dani kayi tafiyarka kawai tunda baka kishin matarka Adnan yanzu so kake a matsayina na matar Aure na dame jikina in fito da surar jikina sabida kawai ka burge abokanan ka suce kai wayayye ne   idan kai Baka San darajata ba bakasan martabar Aure ba ni na sani bazan iya tallatawa abokanan ka jikina ba"



Adnan d'aga kafad'unsa yayi in I don't care manner ya nufi hanyar fita daga d'akin yana "ai ke kanki a tukunya yake some times I do regret how comes some one like me get married to you u are not civilized at all"


Aliya bin bayansa tayi da kallo har ya fice wasu siraren hawaye na zubo mata a k'asan ranta tana mamakin halin Adnan


Adnan wajen da yayi parking motarsa ya nufa adai dai lokacin da Zara ta fito daga b'angarensu sanye da kananan kaya ba ko dankwali tana tauna cingum 

Adnan sakin baki yayi yana kallonta sabida yanda ta masifar tafiya da imaninsa haka yake so yaga Aliya na shiga amma ina idan tayi shigar ma sai ta d'auko wani k'aton Hijab ta saka 

Sirirrin muryar Zara ne ya doki kunnensa "Hi" tace tana d'aga masa Hannu a dai dai lokacin da take wuce shi tare da nufar b'angarensu 


Shima hannu ya daga mata kamar wani soko yace "hi" da idanu ya rakata har ta shige ya sauke wani nannauyan ajiyar zuciya sabida tafiyar ma da ta ringa yi is something else 


A hankali yaja kafafunsa ya nufi cikin motar tare da barin harabar gidan 


Zara kuwa ko sallama batayi ba ta tura k'ofar ta shige aranta kuwa tana admiring d'in Adnan Dan ba karya gayen ya had'u haka take so Amir d'inta ya ringa gayu amma ina ba shida wayewa ko kad'an Abu d'aya biyu sai ya hau wa'azi ita kuwa arayuwarta tana balain San wayayyen mutum 

Ganin bata ga Aliya a palon ba yasa ta nufi bedroom d'inta 

Tura k'ofar tayi ta shige a kwance ta Tarar da Aliya still da kayanta a jikinta dankwallinta kawai ta cire ta ajiye a gefe idonta a lumshe tana d'an karkad'a k'afa shigowar Zara d'akin ne yasa ta bud'e idonta 



"Ya baku tafi dinner tare ba na ganki a kwance"? 

Cewar Zara a dai dai lokacin da take zama a gefen gadon 


Aliya kuwa mik'ewa tayi a hankali fuskarta d'auke da mamakin shigar da Zara tayi duk da ba Yau ta saba ganin Zara da wanan shigar ba amma na Yau ya bata mamaki a matsayinta na matar Aure da kuma tasan basu kadai bane a gidan Dan wani wando tasa 3qtr sai wani matsassen top daya bayyana surar jikinta Kanta kuwa ba dankwalli 


Zara kuwa comfortably ta cigaba da tauna cingum d'inta tana wani irin Abu da ido 


"Zara kina da hankali kuwa kinga yanda kika fito fa amatsayinki na matar Aure and kuma kinsan ba ke kadai ce a gidanan ba yanzu da kun had'u da Adnan fa"



Zara tsaki tayi tace "kina da matsala wallahi Aliya mai aibun shigar danayi so kike Nima na ringa jibga kaya a jikina yanda kike jibgawa bazan iya bafa shikenan Dan ina da Aure sai na takurawa rayuwata wlh bazan iya ba ina mamakin ke da Amir bakuda wayewa ko kad'an wlh  haka kawai a wanan zamanin kayi gayu ma ya ka kare ballantana ma ace kana zaune haka"?


Aliya girgiza kanta tayi kawai Dan tasan Zara tayi nisa  bata jin kira 


" Hmm Zara kenan gaskiya ce dai bazan fasa gaya miki ba wanan ba wayewa bace halaka ce bance karki yi gayu ba kiyi daga ke sai mijinki 
Dan shi ya hallata yaga kwalliyarki ba wani ba amma  bai kamata kiringa wanan shigar kina fitowa ba al halin kinsan bake kadai ce a gidan ba Dan kinga bai kamata Mijina da ba muharraminki bama yaganki da wanan shigar ba Dan haka nidai Dan Allah ko Dan mijina kidaina fitowa ahaka ya kamata kiringa darajar Auren dake kanki kiringa taya mijinki kishin kanki"


Dariya Zara ta kyalkyale dashi harda rik'e ciki tace


"Aliya kenan aure bazai hanani rayuwar Dana Riga na saba dashi ba  babu wani aibu a shigar danake yi babu ruwan wani da rayuwata batun kuma mijinki ni ina ruwana dashi naga nima ina da nawa mijin idan ma yaga irin shigar danayi ya burge shi ke kika jawo Dan kece bakya masa irin shi"


Aliya kallonta kawai take tana hango tsantsar rashin ilimi da rashin hankali a tattare da ita girgiza kanta kawai tayi tace "Allah ya shiryeki Zara nidai kidaina yawo haka sabida bake kadai ce a gidanan ba mijina zai iya ganinki da wanan shigar"

"Na nawa kuma yama gani dan yanzu ma daya zo fita mun gaisa ina ruwanshi dani ke da bazaki ringa masa kwalliya ba ai sai kiyi ta zama ahaka Dan Allah ji wani kaya da kika saka haba Aliya wai yaushe zaki wayene dazu kikace min tare zakuje dinner abokinsa hala sabida wanan manyan kayan da kika sa yak'i tafiya dake kashhh I wish ni nake samun opportunity da kike samu wlh ko hmmm ba a magana"


Aliya tsaki tayi ta koma ta kwanta Dan taga alamar Zara bata da cikakken hankali ahaka Zara ta karaci surutunta sai da ta jiyo horn d'in Mijinta Amir ta mik'e ta fice Aliya kuwa ta bita da add'uar shiriya.




Adnan  kuwa awajen dinner 'yan mata kawai yake bi da kallo Dan rabinsu surorinsu awaje yake nonuwan nan luhum luhum kamar zasu huda Riga su fito sai lashe lebb'e yake wata kawar amarya meera ce ta balain tafiya da imaninsa Dan mazaunanta manya manya ga hips tommynta very flat shape d'inta yafita sosai duk da bata kamo Aliyarsa a kyau ba amma fa ta tafi da imaninsa sosai 


Ahaka ya ringa monitoring d'inta da ido idan ta shiga filin rawa sai ya tashi ya mata 
 Liki sai yanzu yake godewa Allah da Aliya bata biyo shi ba 


Meera kuwa tuni itama taji Adnan ya kwanta mata arai 


Adnan bai sha wahalar shawo kanta ba har ya amshi numberta wajen sha biyun dare aka fara watsewa daga wajen dinner inda Adnan ya bukaci zai kai ta gida sai  da tayi insisting yayi dropping kawayenta tukuna kafin ya kai ta gida haka kuwa akayi sai da yafara kai kawayenta ya nufi gida da ita suna tafe suna hira jefi jefi yana juyowa yana karewa nonanta kallo Dan shi yafi tsone masa ido kamar zasu fito daga rigar Dan da alama ita kanta meera da k'yar take numfashi sabida matseta da kayan sukayi


A hankali adnan ya d'auki d'aya hannunsa ya ruk'o mata hannu yana murzawa ita kuwa meera sai murmushi take hakan da Adnan ya gani ne yasa ya maida hannunsa cinyarta yafara shafawa ganin bata hanasa ba yasa shi kuwa yacigaba da yawo da hannayensa a jikinta 

Suna dab da k'arasowa k'ofar gidansu tace yayi parking ga gidansu can 

Parking yayi ya cigaba da kallonta kafin ta Ankara ya jawota ya rungume ya kaiwa nononta chapka Abun mamaki ko k'ok'arin hana shi bata yi ba nikuwa nace dama akan mai zata hana shi matar da tayi tallan surar jikinta babu ko mayafi ai ko ba'a fad'a ba ansan babu mutunci a tattare da ita 


Sai data ga yana Neman wucewa gona da iri ta janye jikinta 

.Adnan kuwa ya d'ago a rikice yana ta taimakesa ya samu nutsuwa mak'e kafad'arta tayi tace dare yayi gobe yazo su had'u kuma idan zai zo ya ciko Aljihunsa


Murmushi yayi yace mata angama  a lokacin data ke k'ok'arin fita daga motar yasa hannu a Aljihunsa ya kirgo dubu biyar ya bata tayi mishi godiya yaja motarsa ya tafi.



   Zara kuwa tana barin b'angarensu Aliya kicibus tayi da Amir a bakin k'ofar shiga b'angarenta 
Baki ya saki yana binta da kallo tundaga sama har k'asa 

Zara bata damu da irin kallon da yake binta dashi ba ta tafi da gudu zata rungumeshi 


Tureta yayi da k'arfi ta fad'i k'asa yace "Zara wai yaushe zakiyi hankali ne da aurena akanki kike fita ahaka al halin kinsan bamu kadai ne a gidan ba haka kika ga makociyarki nayi ?ban tab'a ganinta ba hijabi ba wlh amma ke dayake bakisan darajar kanki ba bakinsan darajar Aure ba shine zaki fita ahaka da yanzu mijinta ya ganki ahaka fa?.........*M🅰K'OT🅰N JUN🅰*

*Page 2*


Zara wani irin wulakantacen kallo take binshi dashi ta mik'e tare da Jan wani dogon tsaki ta shige ta bar shi a tsaye 


Amir sai da ya samu minti biyar a tsaye yana mamakin halin Zara dan bata da mutunci ko kad'an batayi regarding d'insa as husband ba hasali ma babu Wanda ta raina sama dashi bai isa yasa doka tabi ba tun ba aje ko'ina ba yafara Dana sanin aurenta Wanda dudu auren nasu baifi 5month ba ko dayake bata fito mishi Da zahirin halinta ba sai da ya aureta Dan kafin ya aureta tana gudar abinda zai bata mishi rai idan ba mantawa yayi ba har Hijabi take sawa duk dan ta faranta mishi rai duk da yasan she is use to English wears dan duka gidansu haka suke yan boko hatta mahaifinta har gobe yana tsukewa da Riga da wando


Girgiza kansa yayi tare da nema mata shiriya wajen Allah ya shige ciki


A Palo ya Tarar da ita a kwance sai girgiza k'afa take fuskar nan  a turb'une 


Amir bai ce mata komai ba  ya wuce d'aki ita kuwa ta bishi da harara da tsaki

Sai da ya watsa ruwa yasaka kaya marasa nauyi ya fito ya nufi dining dan yaci abinci


Zara dai na zaune yanda ya barta sai dai wanan Karin tana danne danne a waya tana ta murmushi ita kad'ai


Warmers  biyun dake ajiye akan dining  ya bud'e to his suprise babban cornflakes ne a ciki k'aramin kuma sugar da milk

A hankali ya d'ago yana kallon Zaran dake ta dariya ita kad'ai sai kace mahaukaciya dan har ta zamo daga kan kujerar

D'aukar warmers d'in yayi ya nufi inda take rai a b'ace yace "Zara what is this"?


Zara ko d'agowa bata yi ba tace " what did u see"?

"Zara menene wanan don't let me loss my anger"

Tsaki Zara tayi ta k'ara relaxing jikinta ajikin kujerar tace "dinner d'inka ne idan bazaka iyaci ba dan Allah ka ajiye banasan disturbance am having a fun here"

"Is this wat u called food Zara kirasa mai zaki ajiye min sai cornflakes Zara wai meyesa kike min haka ne mai kika d'auki Aurene Zara bakida burin daya wuce ki bak'anta min rai a gidanan kowa yana Aure dan ya huta amma Zara ni sai kuntatta min kikeyi ko d'an indomie bazaki iya dafawa ki ajiye min ba"


Zara katse shi tayi da sauri tana bubbuga k'afarta a k'asa "wai nikam Amir meyesa idan baka takura min ba baka jin dadi we don't take heavy food at night ban taso a gidanmu naga ana cin abinci mai nauyi daddare ba cornflakes muke ci ko muci biscuits dats y na ajiye maka idan kasan bazaka ci ba pls kindly get into the kitchen and get some thing for yourself"

Daga haka ta mik'e tana k'ok'arin wucewa ta gefensa 


Amir fusgota yayi har sai da top d'in jikinta ya d'an yage rai a mutuk'ar b'ace yace " this is not your fathers house Zara this is my house dan gidanku ba acin abincin mai Nauyi ni gidana ana ci daga yau wlh idan kika k'ara ajiye min irin wanan abun wlh sai na miki abinda bantab'a miki ba since morning I have not taking anything na dawo kuma ki tareni da irin wanan abincin sabida bakida mutunci naga alamar na sakar miki dayawa zan fara d'aukar mataki akanki"

Tureta yayi ta fadi kan kujerar ya barta baki abud'e har sai da ya shige kitchen dan ya samawa kansa abinda zai ci 


Kukan takaici Zara ta fashe dashi dan this is the first time da Amir yayi mata haka and sai ta rama is a promise take taji haushi da tsanarsa duk ya rufeta tafara Dana sanin aurensa ita da take da samari masu Santa amma ta biyewa zuciyarta ta auri Amir dan duk a samarinta shi tafiso yakuma fisu kyau da abinda zai saka mata kenan banda takura mata dayake yi da uban dokokinsa duk abinda tayi sai ya mata fad'a he is not civilized at all 

Sai da tayi kuka mai isanta ta mik'e ta nufi bedroom d'inta tana kissima irin rashin mutuncin da zata mishi 


Amir kuwa indomie ya dafa da kwai yaci ya koshi shima ya nufi bedroom d'insa batare da ya kalli d'akin Zara ba 


Zara kuwa tana shigewa d'aki ta cire kayan jikinta ta watsa ruwa tazo ta kwanta ta janyo wayarta ta fara chattn da Saleem Smart tsohon saurayinta dan duk da tayi Aure bata fasa kula shi ba shima hakan dan wani zubin har waya su nayi indai Amir ya b'ata mata rai Saleem d'in ne ya deb'e mata kewa a Daren da hirarrakin da basu dace ba amatsayinta na matar Aure sai wajen uku bacci ya d'auketa


Amir kuwa baccinsa yayi hankali kwance batare da ya wani bari damuwar Zara ta sha masa kai ba.





Adnan kuwa ba shi ya dawo gidan ba sai wajen d'ayan Dare a lokacin Aliya har tayi shirin bacci tayi kwanciyarta amma ba bacci take ba idonta biyu tana jinjina halin Adnan ace magidanci har d'ayan dare bai dawo ba ahaka Adnan ya shiga d'akin ya sameta 

"Ya dai mai kikeyi bakiyi bacci ba har yanzu"?

Maganar daya fito daga bakin Adnan kenan 

Aliya mik'ewa tayi daga kwance da take ta gayyato fara'a a fuskarta duk da ranta a b'ace yake 

" Ya za'ayi nayi bacci baka dawo ba"

Adnan dake rage kayan jikinsa cewa yayi "ai da baki jirani ba kinyi baccinki"

Daga haka ya nufi band'aki dan yayi wanka 

Aliya bin bayansa tayi da kallo har ya shige tana zaune ya fito daga bandakin ya zo ya zura Riga ya tadda Sallah 

Komawa tayi ta kwanta ta zuba masa ido har ya iddar da sallar ya mik'e ya cire rigarsa ya d'auki wayarsa yayi waje

Aliya kuwa a tunaninta Abu zai d'auko a Palo ya dawo ya kwanta mik'ewa tayi ta k'ara feshe jikinta da turare ta dawo ta kwanta tana jiran shigowarsa 


Adnan kuwa yana fita Palo ya turawa Meera text akan ta hau watsapp suyi magana yayi kwanciyarsa akan 3 seater tare da kunna data 

Aliya ganin Adnan bai shigo d'akin ba har  tsawon minti goma ne yasa ta mik'e ta nufi Palo dan taga mai yake yi 


Tana bud'e k'ofar d'akin ta hango shi a kwance da waya a hannu yana murmushi shi kadai daga shi Sai gajeren wando yayi zurfi a chattn d'in da yake yi 

Mamaki ne ya rufeta ta kai kallonta kan agogon dake palon taga 1:20 take ranta ya k'ara b'aci ta nufi wajen Adnan 


Adai dai lokacin da yake zooming hoton kirjin Meera hankalinsa a tashe dan har joy stick d'insa ya mik'e 


Agabansa Aliya ta tsaya tana binshi da kallo inda ta tsayar da idonta akan joy stick d'insa dake tsaye  


Adnan  da sauri ya mik'e daga kwancen da yake dan bayason Aliya taga halin da yake ciki bai san ya riga da ya makaro ba duk da yayi sauri ya d'auki pillo ya d'ora akan k'afarsa ajiye wayar yayi a gefe yace "wai bakya jin bacci ne kije ki kwanta mana ni ba yanzu zanyi bacci ba Sai anjima yanzu ina chattn da Muhammad ne"


Aliya controlling anger d'inta tayi ta zauna a gefensa tace "Adnan meyesa kake min hakane akwai wani Abu da nake maka ne da baka so na daina u don't care for me bana gabanka at all duk abinda nake yi dan na burgeka Adnan baka gani yanzu akan ka kwanta dani a d'aki gwara ka fito Palo kayi chattn yanzu wajen sati d'aya kenan baka yi making love dani ba pls idan akwai abinda nayi maka ka gayamin na baka hakuri" 


Adnan dafe kansa yayi yace "nifa Aliya babu abinda kika min kawai dai agajiye nake ne kwanakin nan wlh am not in the mood of all this but don't worry I will make it up to you idan nayi clearing abunda ke gabana ke daina d'aukan abinda bai taka k'ara ya karya ba kiringa sa shi a ranki kije kiyi kwanciyarki ina nan shigowa


Aliya hawayen da take ta b'oyewa ne ya hau zubo mata dan tasan karya yake tasan irin mijin da ta Aura bata so ta fara zargin shi dan ko ta d'auko hanyar da zata zarge shi Sai tayi sauri ta cire hakan a zuciyarta ta ringa kyautata mishi zato  bata San mai yasa baya nunawa ya damu da ita ba yanzu idan zata kwana a kirjinsa half naked ko ajikinsa yanzu taga fa halin da yake ciki alamar yana bukatar mace amma bazai nemeta ba ko dai akwai wacce take d'auke masa hankaline awaje bata sani ba 


Ganin ya d'auki wayarsa yana cigaba da chattn d'in da yake yine yasa ta mik'e ta nufi d'akin ta 


Adnan kuwa ko ajikinsa dan ya Riga da yayi nisa yanda zai samu Meera kawai yake nema Sai da ya kai  ukun dare yana chtn da Meera hankalinsa duk a tashe dan sunyi musayar nude pictures sosai Allah Allah yake gari ya waye su had'u ya d'anyi ridding d'inta 


A lokacin da ya koma d'akin Har bacci yayi awon gaba da Aliya agefenta ya kwanta shima nan da nan bacci ya d'auke shi.


Washegari da sassafe Aliya ta kamalla aikinta kamar yanda ta saba ta caba ado ta koma d'aki tana jiran Adnan ya tashi dan ko sallar Asuba dak'yar ta samu yayi 


Adnan kuwa juyi kawai yake yana mafarki da Meera acikin baccin yafara wani irin nishi yana zillo shi kad'ai Aliya dake kwance a kujerar d'akin ta zuba mishi ido tana kallon ikon Allah mamaki kuma a dank'are aranta 


Adnan kuwa duk ya d'auke gaske ne Sai da ya b'ata wandonsa ya bud'e idonsa aikuwa suka had'a ido da Aliya 


Da Sauri ya mik'e yana mitsitsika idonsa 

Aliya kuwa ta d'auke kanta zuciyarta na mata suya tana kuma tunanin ko mafarkin waye ke 

Adnan da borin kunya ya mik'e ya nufi wajenta ya sunkuya agaban kujerar da take yana kamo hannunta 

Aliya kuwa ta zuba mishi ido kamar mai San ta gano wani Abu a kwayar idonsa 

" I was just dreaming we are making love and look at" yace yana nuna mata wandonsa 


Aliya bata ce komai ba illa kau da kanta da tayi

Shi kuwa Adnan ya cigaba da janta da wasa ganin bai samu fuska yasa ya mik'e ya nufi band'aki dan yayi wanka 


Aliya kuwa tunanin ta hau yi na abubuwan dake faruwa dan tasan Adnan mutum ne mai sha'awa kuma tana iya k'ok'arinta wajen ganin ta jawo hankalinsa gareta dai dai gwargwado tana kyautata masa kwaliyan nan ta nayi amma ba tasan mai yasa  bata gabanshi idan dai dagasken yayi mafarki da ita mai yasa bazai neme ta a zahiri ba ko har ta gundure shi ne da can ba haka Adnan yake ba farko farkon Aurensu daga baya taga ya fara canja mata 


Ahaka tayi zurfi a tunani har Adnan ya fito ya shirya cikin kananan Kaya ya fice daga d'akin karar rufe k'ofar d'akin ne ya dawo da ita daga duniyar tunanin data tafi


Mik'ewa tayi tafito daga d'akin taga Adnan har ya zauna awajen dining yana waya k'arasawa tayi tayi serving d'inshi itama ta zuba nata ta fara ci batare da ta jira shi ba 


Shima yana gama wayar yafara ci batare da ya kalleta ba dan shi yama fisan haka idan tana share shi yafi  sakin jiki ya  tsula tsiyarsa  


Babu abinda kake jin kararsa in banda karar cokula Aliya kuwa kanta a sunkuye suna dab da gama cin abincin Suka ji ana kwankwasa k'ofa kafin Aliya tayi wani yunkuri Har Adnan ya mik'e yace "don't worry let me check who is on the door"

Aliya kuwa abincinta ta cigaba da ci tana tunanin ko mai musu wanki ne dan jiya ta had'a mishi wankin Adnan


Adnan kuwa yana bud'e k'ofar yaga Zara a tsaye ta wani gantsaro kirji tana hamma sleeping dress ne ajikinta doguwar Riga har k'asa mai tsantsi yabi ya lafe ajikinta kana hango komai na jikinta duk da rigar ma mai dogon hannu ne ta saka wani hular net 


Adnan wani irin yawu ya hadiya a lokacin dayabi kirjinta dake tsaye carko Carko a tsaye duk da bata kamo Aliya kirji ba


Ita kuwa Zara gaishe shi tayi tana k'ok'arin wucewa ta gefensa 

Adai dai lokacin da Aliya ta taso tana so taga waye dan taga yanda Adnan ya kame awaje d'aya 


Amir shima adai dai lokacin ya fito dan yatafi office dan ko d'akin Zara bai kalla ba dan mugun haushinta yake ji 


Ai kamar cewa akayi ya juya ya kalli b'angaren Su Adnan ya hango Zara na k'ok'arin shigewa a rikice ya juya ya nufi wajen nasu ransa a mutuk'ar b'ace dan baisan rashin hankalin Zara ya kai tafito ahaka ba shi kuma Adnan na tsaye yana kare mata kallo 


Da wani irin murya ya kwalla mata kira "Zaraaaa" 


Adai dai lokacin da itama Aliya zuciya ya deb'eta ta kwashe Zara da mari sabida takaicin yanda tafito........*M🅰K'OT🅰N JUN🅰*

"Bansan dabbancinki da tumasancinki da rashin kamun kan naki ya kai ki fito tsirara kizo ki tsaya agaban namijin da ba muharraminki ba ko kishin kanki bakya yi"


Aliya tace tana huci Zara kuwa cikin tsananin mamaki take kallonta hannunta dafe da k'uncinta 

Adnan kuwa da sauri ya matsa daga hanya daya hango yanda Ameer ke tahowa idon nan jajjur 



Zara dake k'ok'arin magana wani irin finciko ta Ameer yayi daga bakin k'ofa yafara janta da k'asa tana ihu 


Aliya kuwa wani irin kallo take bin Adnan da shi 

Adnan kuwa dan k'arfin hali ya zabga mata harara ya fice dasauri 

Sai daya had'a da gudu ya iya taddo Amir Dan har sun kusa Shiga b'angarensu still yana Jan Zara da k'asa a fusace Dan har gashin kanta ya damka

"Enoughhhh" Ameer yace a tsawace jijjiyoyin kansa a tattashe idanan a rine ba Zara ba hatta Adnan sai daya tsorata abunka da mai hakuri bai iya fushi ba


"Kace zuwa kayi ka cigaba da kare mata kallo baka gaji da kallonta ba ko kunya bakaji ba ka tsaya kana karewa matar Aure kallo ban tab'a tunanin akuyancin naka zai kai har akan matata ba daga yau babu ruwanka da shiga sabgar data shafeni matata ce idan naga dama na kasheta babu abinda ya dameka"

Daga haka ya Cigaba da Jan Zara a k'asa ya shige tare da rufo k'ofarsa yana shiga ya zare belt d'insa yafara tapkawa Zara tana tsalla Ihu sai zagaye palon suke sai da ya mata lilis ya wurgar da belt d'in ya zube akan kujera yana sauke ajiyar zuciya wani irin tafasa zuciyarsa ke yi dan shi namijine mai balain kishi 


Adnan kuwa tunda ya fara jiyo ihun Zara yaji kamar ya b'alle k'ofar ya shige sai da ya daina jin alamar ana bugunta ya nufi b'angarensa yana mai balain jin haushin Marin da Aliya tayiwa Zara 



A Palo ya tarar da Aliya tana ta kai da kawowa sai share hawaye take 


Adnan kuwa hakan da ya gani ne yasa yaja dogon tsaki ya nufi d'akinsa Dan ya d'auko brief case d'insa ya tafi


Aliya da sauri ta sha gabansa cikin rawar murya tace "Adnan abinda kayi kana ganin ka kyauta Kenan ka zubawa matar aure ido ko kiftawa bakayi saii"


Tsawan da Adnan ya daka mata ne yasa tayi sauri ta hadiye maganar da take yi 


"Uban mai nayi an gaya miki kowa irin ki ne kidahuma shikenan Dan tana matar Aure sai na rufe idona ko zaki iya gaya min uban me tayi miki kika mareta sabida ta fito da sleeping dress an gaya miki ita irinki ce macece ita wayaya ba irinki ba ke kiyi ta fama da zani da atamfa kina kunshe kanki a hijabi kina wari"


Jikin Aliya in banda rawa babu abinda yake yi duk kalaman dake fitowa a bakinsa kamar narkakken dalma yake watsa mata hawaye kuwa sai ambaliya yake a kumatunta 


Adnan tsaki yayi ya ratsa ta gefenta ya d'auko breifcase d'insa ya fice. 

Aliya kuwa  durk'ushe tayi a  gurin tare da fashewa  da matsanacin kuka tana rik'e zuciyarta sabida tsananin zafin da yake mata 


Ameer kuwa sai da ya samu nutsuwa sosai ya d'ago da rinannun idonsa ya nufi wajen Zara dake sheme a k'asa tana kuka 

Gashin kanta ya damko ta saki wani wahallalan ihu dan muryarta bata fita Sam sabida tsabar ihun da ta ringayi fatar ta tayi rudu rudu 


"Wlh daga yau ko k'ofar gida kika k'ara fita ba tare da Hijabi ba wlh dukan da na miki yau kad'an ne shegiya 'yar akuya da bata San martabar Aure ba  daga yau ma na k'ara ganin kin saka kananan kaya wlh sai na Lahira yafiki jin dadi useless human being"

Daga haka ya sakar mata kanta tare da hambare ta 

"Allah ya isa tsakanina dakai Ameer na tsaneka bana kaunarka ai Auren ba dole bane kabani takardata bazan iya cigaba da zama da mahaukaci da bai San darajar mace ba kuma wlh kananan kaya yanzu nafara sawa sai dai ka kasheni"

Zara tace tana fashewa da matsanacin kuka 


Ameer ko kallonta baiyi ba yafice daga palon yaje ya hau motarsa tare da dannawa Adnan dake ta k'ok'arin tada motarsa harara ya fice daga gidan


Adnan kuwa maida kallonsa yayi b'angarensu Ameer bayan Ameer yabar harabar gidan haka kawai zuciyarsa ta ringa raya mishi abubuwa 

Yana daga cikin mota ya juya ya kalli b'angarensu ganin k'ofar a rufe kamar yanda yake ne yasa ya fito daga motar ya rufe kansa tsaye ya nufi b'angarensu Amir ba tare da wani tsoro ko shakka ba 


A hankali ya tura k'ofar shiga b'angaren yaga ya bud'u dayake ginin iri d'ayane bai sha wahalar gane palon gidan ba tun kafin ya k'arasa yafara jiyo sheshek'ar kukan Zaran da sauri ya shiga palon ya hangota a kwance a k'asa tana ta kuka 


Zara da Sauri ta d'ago da kanta sakamakon k'amshin turaren Adnan da taji ya ziyarci hancinta 


Aikuwa kuka ta k'ara fashewa dashi da taga alamar tausayinta a kwance a fuskarsa 

Adnan kuwa a hankali ya k'arasa kusa da ita ya tsunguna  agabanta ya zaro hankici daga Aljihunsa ya mik'a mata yana mata sannu da d'aya hannun nasa


Zara kuwa kukan ta k'ara fashewa dashi ba tare da ta mik'a hannunta ta k'arbi Hankicin ba 

Cikin sanyi murya da tausayawa Adnan ya fara magana yana "kiyi hakuri Zara ki tashi ki share hawayenki ban tab'a tunanin Mijinki Jahili bane sai yau Idan ba haka ba banga abinda kikayi da kika cancanci irin wanan dukan ba Wanda bashi da ilimi ai shi yake dukan mace pls bar kukan haka kar kanki yayi ciwo k'arbi ki share hawayenki gani nayi bazan iya tafiya ba tare da naga halin da kike ciki ba"


Tunda yafara magana Zara ta kura mishi ido abubuwa nata mata yawo ajiki muryarshi ba k'aramin sanyi yasa tafara ji aranta ba Adnan shine irin mijin daya dace da ita he is social and a caring type 


"Pls k'arbi ki share hawayenki"

Adnan ya k'ara cewa yana mik'a mata hankicin hannunsa


Zara a hankali ta mik'a masa hannunta ya bata hankicin tafara goge fuskarta still hawaye na ambaliya a fuskarta 

"Sorry" Adnan yace yana k'yab'e fuskarsa kamar zaiyi kuka 

"Thank you"

Zara tace tana zuba mishi ido 

Adnan mik'ewa yayi yace "pls kisamu ruwan zafi ki gasa jikinki kinga am not the right person to do all this da ni zan gasa miki jikin naki pls idan kin gasa jikin naki kisamu magani kisha"

Zara ji tayi kamar ta dakka tsalle ta rungume shi sabida wani mugun sansa daya shigeta farat d'aya 


Cikin muryarta da baya fita tace "pls ka taimaka min na tashi bazan iya tashi ba"

Adnan mik'a mata hannunsa yayi ta rik'e take su biyu suka ji wani irin shock ya ziyarce su Adnan kuwa ya mik'ar da ita yana karewa kirjinta kallo "thank you am greatfull " 

Zara tace tana k'ara kura mishi ido 

Adnan murmushi ya sakar mata yace "u are wlcm"

Zaro idonsa yayi a lokacin dayaga yanda fatar jikinta yayi rudu rudu a kumbure dan hatta hannayenta a kukumbure suke 


"Subhanallahi yanzu haka Ameer yaji miki ciwo"

Ai tamkar zuga Zara yayi ta fashe da kuka tana "wlh jikina duk ciwo yake min da belt d'insa ya dakeni dubi fa irin ciwon dayaji min"

Zara tace a lokacin da take zame rigarta ta sama 

Numfashin Adnan ne ya kusa d'aukewa na wucin gadi sakamakon tozali da yayi da surar Zara ita kuwa Zara kuka ta cigaba da yi tana nununa masa inda Ameer yaji mata ciwo harda d'aga rigarta sama cinyarta ya bayyana Adnan kuwa wani irin yawu ya k'ara hadiyewa dayaga santala santalan cinyarta awaje 

(Readers Zara fa bata kamo Aliya a diri da kyau bafa dan tafi Zara komai sai dai Zara ta gaya mata tsayi da haske dan ita fara ce kal kal sabanin Aliya da bata kai ta haske ba amma kuwa duk baiwar da Allah yayi wa matarsa baya gani idan namiji maneman mata ne wlh matarsa ko da gwal aka kerata bazata tab'a burgeshi ba sai dai na haram d'in Allah ya shirya mana mazajenmu wayanda kuma suke Neman matan banza Allah ya shirya mana su)


Cikin stammering Adnan yace "amma bai kyauta ba sorry"

Zara d'agowa tayi tace "ngd bari na shiga toilet na gasa jikin nawa amma fa bansan mai nayiwa matarka ta tsinka min mari ba dazu am really hurt mai dan nafito ahaka did she think am trying to seduce you or wat"?

Adnan cewa yayi " karki damu na d'au mataki akanta kinsan kidahuma ce bata da wayewa ko kad'an"

Adnan ai ji yayi kamar ya fusgota ya rungume sabida ta gama tafiya da imaninsa shima wani kwarton kallo yabita da shi daga sama har k'asa yace "bari na tafi nayi latti a office oll because of you"

Jiki na rawa ya mik'a mata hannu ta kuwa rik'e gam sai da suka kurawa juna ido na second goma Zara ta janye hannunta tayi mai bye da hannu ya sakar mata murmushi ya juya zai bar palon

"Pls can I have your contact"? 

Yaji muryar Zara ta doki kunnensa 


Juyawa yayi yace "of course"

Dingisawa tayi ta d'auko wayarta daga d'aki Adnan kuwa sai hadiyar yawu yake dan duk taku d'aya sai mazaunanta sun motsa 


Lambarsa ya shigar mata ya mik'a mata tayi saving ta k'ara ce mishi ta gode Adnan ya sakar mata murmushi ya juya ya tafi yana zuwa k'ofar fita daga palon sai da ya k'ara juyowa ya sakar mata murmushin da ya saukar mata da kasala da wani sanyi lokaci guda ya fice yarap ta zube akan kujera ta lumshe idonta tana ayyana abubuwa a ranta ko ciwon da jikinta keyi bata ji 


Adnan kuwa sai da ya d'an lab'e ya kalli b'angarensu yaga alamar babu kowa ya nufi motarsa da sauri ya tayar ya fice daga gidan 


Aliya kuwa kuka tayi sosai Wanda ya haddasa mata ciwon kai da zazzabi a take 


rarrafawa tayi ta shige d'aki ta kwanta duk maganganun Adnan na mata amsa kuwwa a kunne idan ta tuna warin dayace tana yi sai ta kara rushewa da kuka.

Yinin Ranar da tunanin Zara Adnan ya wuni sabida tunaninta ma ya manta da wata Meera sai data kirashi ya tunata duk dokin dayake na su had'u sai yaji baya wani dokin su had'un 


Dagawa yayi yace zai kirata nan da k'arfe hud'u 


Aikuwa sai da ta k'ara kiranshi ya tuna tsaki yaja yace mata su had'u a Horizon hotel zaije ya musu booking yanzu banza banza ya rage zafi 


Karfe  biyar da rabi yana kwance a d'akin da ya kama musu daga shi sai gajeren wando yana jiran zuwan Meera 


Meera kuwa ba wani Abu bane yasa ta matsu su had'u da Adnan sai dan cikon kud'in ankon kawarta da take nema dan asanadin karya da gasa da San yin ankon masifa  ta rasa budurcinta tundaga lokacin indai anko aka fito da shi ko nawane sai tayi  inta kama ma tabi maza ukune arana


Room number da Adnan ya gaya mata tashiga ganin Adnan yayi wani rashe rashe daga shi sai gajeran wando yasa ta sakar masa murmushi shima ya sakar mata yana mai kare mata kallo sai yanzu yake hango muninta dan ganin dare ya mata gashi ta sha fente fente

"Dama ke karuwace"?

Shine kalmar daya fito daga bakin Adnan 

" ni ba karuwa bace amma kasan dai babu yanda zakayi ka hayeni abanza"

"To ai karuwai ke ciniki kafin a dannesu amma karki damu ni ba mitsiyaci bane babu yanda za'ayi na gurjeki a banza ba tare da na miki ihsani ba dafatan ba sai nasa protector ba hope am save"


Yace yana nuna mata condom d'in dake ajiye a bedside d'in d'akin 

"Gaskiya kasa dan jiya na gama period"

Meera tace rai a d'an bace dan taji haushin maganar da ya mata

*Wanan shafin nakine ke kadai kiyi yanda kikeso dashi Maman Basheer Ngd sosai da so da K'auna*


*page 4*
           
         Adnan mitsitsika idonsa yayi sosai dan ya ya tabbatar da kwantacen nonon daya gani mai kama da kulin sigan Ashirin 


Meera kuwa jikinta ne ya d'an fara rawa ta janyo rigarta tana k'ok'arin kare kirjinta 


Adnan kuwa daya zama so shock da sauri ya janye rigar ya k'ara wurgar dashi yace "ke mai wanan? Yasa hannunsa yana kamo tatsitsiyen nononta dake kwance kamar jikakken tsuma sabida tsabar matsar daya sha 

Doke hannunsa Meera tayi tace " Wai mai hakane wani rashin mutuncin ne haka"?

"Wane irin rashin mutunci Ameera ya zan kwadaitu da surar jikinki amma Ashe ciko kike yi ba nakin bane ni zaki yaudara karma kice min baya da hips d'in shima cikone"


Ya janyota yana k'ok'arin rabata da skirt d'inta 

Meera kuwa data ga Abun Adnan rashin mutinci ne k'ok'awa ta fara yi dashi da hannu d'aya tana rufe kirjinta da hannu d'aya 

Adnan kuwa a zuciye ya wanka mata mari ya had'e hannayenta acinyarsa ya zame mata skirt d'in Meera kuwa sai mutsu mutsu take 

Bak'in skiny dake jikinta ya zare yaga still akwai wani tight skinny ajikinta aikuwa ya zare shi da k'arfi sai ga linkakkun towels guda biyu data yi hips dashi sun fad'o sai wani form mai zubin pant data saka ya taimaka wajen saka duwawun nata tudu


Adnan sakinta yayi yana sallati dan bai tab'a tunanin Meera is shapeless ba Ashe duk yaudara ce mugun acuci take yi 

Meera kuwa kukan takaici ta fashe dashi dan bata tab'a had'uwa da dan iska Mara mutunci kamar Adnan ba dan duk wayanda take bi basa wani damuwa idan suka gano ciko tayi burinsa dai su samu biyan bukatarsu da ita amma sai gashi Adnan ya mata dalla dalla 

"Yanzu dama ameera  ciko kike yi kike yaudarar mutane da wanan bushashen jikin naki har kike fad'ar baayi ciniki ba amma ban tab'a ganin 'yar iska irinki ba kuma ahaka kike samun masu kusantarki nono kamar lagwani ni ban ma tab'a ganin duwawu Mara tudu kamar faranti ba sai naki" 

Adnan yace yana kaiwa duwawunta duka 

Meera kuwa babu abinda take yi in banda kuka dan karshen cin mutunci Adnan yayi mata 

Mik'ewa tayi a hankali tana share hawaye tabar kan gadon ta nufi inda kayanta ke zube dan ta saka 


Adnan da sauri ya duro daga kan gadon yasha gabanta  yace "ba tambayarki nake ba uban meyesa kike ciko dan ki yaudari mutane "


Meera kuwa kuka ta fashe dashi sosai ta had'e hannayenta tace "Dan Allah dan annabi ka kyalleni nasaka kayana na tafi cin mutunci ma kawai daka min ya isheni haka dan Allah ka rabu dani daga haka ta fara saka kayanta 


Adnan kuwa kame kugu kawai yayi yana kallon ko ina na jikinta da mamaki dan wlh badan ya tub'eta ba ko hadiyar Qurani akayi akace mishi ciko tayi karyatawa zaiyi yanda shape d'inta ya fita 

Bra d'inta ta saka ba tare da tasa acucin ba har towel d'in maidasu tayi cikin jakarta tana ta share hawaye 

Adnan kuwa ya nufi wajen ta da sauri ya rik'e mata hannun da ta rik'e towels d'in zata sa a ajaka 


" kinsan Allah idan kika ga na barki kin tafi salin Alin ba tare da na ci ubanki ba sabida yaudarar da kikamin wlh sai kin saka duk cikon da kikayi agabana naga yanda kike yi"

Meera zuba mishi ido kawai tayi tana kallonsa hawaye nata tsiyayya a idonta sai ayau taji ta tsani barikin da take yi sai ayau tayi nadamar halin da ta jefa kanta ji wani irin wulakanci da Adnan yake mata har dasu Mari 


Adnan kuwa d'akin yaje ya kulle yace idan bata saka acucin ba sai dai su kwana ahaka ya fara soka mata Ashar 


Meera ganin babu sarki sai Allah ta sunkuya ta zazzago da kayan da take ciko dasu tafara sawa cikin kwarewa 


Adnan kuwa kur ya mata da ido yana bala'in mamakin yanda ta sake kayan cikon cikin kwarewa bra d'in ba k'aramin ciko tayi masa ba kaf'in ya taso yayi tuntsin tuntsin kamar gaske 

Cikin minti biyar Meera ta dawo mace mai shape sosai ta sunkuya ta d'auki mayafinta still tana sharce hawaye 
Cikin muryar Kuka tace "yanzu zan iya tafiya"?

Adnan dak'yar ya rufe bakinsa dan mamaki yace " Amma Allah ya isanmu wlh Ashe ahaka kuke cutar maza har nafara tausayin namijin da tsautsayi zai kai shi yace zai Aureki kinsan Allah baki isa kici dubu biyar d'in Dana baki jiya a banza ba na d'auka ke mace Ce Ashe fuskar mata gareki jikin na maza dan wlh ko ni nan nafiki mazaunai kirjina idan na had'eshi waje d'aya zai ma fi naki banda wahalar dani da kikayi ma jiya na ringa. Kai kawayenki gida maza  kafin na kirga biyar ki bani kudina dana baki jiya 


Tunda ya fara magana Meera kukan data ke ya tsaya cak ta kura mishi ido tana mai balain mamakin Rashin mutuncin da yake kuma shirin yi mata

Adnan mik'ewa yayi ya d'auki Jakarta ta tafi da gudu zata k'arba tana "haba dan Allah mai nayi maka haka kake min irin wanan wulakancin da cin mutunci"?

Adnan tureta yayi ya fito da kud'in Jakarta harda sababbin kud'in da aka lik'a awajen bikin jiya ya kirga dubu biyar acikin kud'in kuma da alama dama iya kudinsa ne ajakarta sai 'yan chanjin kud'in likin 


Meera wani mugun kuka ta fashe dashi dan gabad'aya kud'in jakarta dubu takwas ne cikon dubu goma take nema sai gashi ya kwashe kud'in daya bata 

" Allah ya isa tsakanina dakai Wlh ba zan tab'a yafe maka ba abinda kayimin sai anyiwa 'yarka insha Allah 


Adnan kuwa dariya kawai yake yi yana saka kayansa sai daya gama sakawa yace "na barki lafiya aji tsoron Allah a daina a cuci ya za'ayi banci nanun ba nanun ya cini hauka ake yau ma sai nayi mugun mafarki sakamakon wanan bushashen jikin naki Dana kalla nima Allah ya isa ban yafe miki ba sakamakon rashin baccin da ban samu ba jiya 'yar iska Mara mutunci idan kinga dama kitafi idan baki ga dama ba kiyi ta zama ni kinga tafiyata"


Meera da zagi da tsinuwa ta bishi har ya fice daga d'akin ita kuwa ta zube a k'asa tana mai cigaba da kuka.




B'angaren Zara kuwa wunin ranar tunanin Adnan ta ringa yi babu abinda take hangowa sai irin Sexy look d'in da yaringa binta dashi da murmushinsa mai sanyaya zuciya tunanin Adnan ya taimaka wajen rage mata zafi da rad'ad'in dukan da  Ameer ya mata kamshin turaren Adnan dake cikin hankicin da ya bata take ta shak'a tana lumshe ido shaidan na ta k'awata mata Dacewarta da Adnan akan Ameer.

Aliya kuwa a daddafe take iya mik'ewa tayi sallah sabida zazzafan zazzabin daya rufeta kanta kamar ya rab'e gida biyu ahaka ta wuni a d'aki ko abinci bata iya d'orawa ba 


Wajen k'arfe biyar na Yamma Adnan ya dawo gida musamman dan yaga Zara dan sosai yake jin sha'awar Zara babu abinda ke masa yawo a ido sai surarta da santala santalan cinyarta har wayarsa ya ringa dubawa yaga ko ta kirashi  yasan Ameer sai dare yake dawowa Tara ko goma shiyasa yayi amfani da wanan damar wajen dawowa da Wuri 

 bai shigar da motarsa ciki ba yayi parking a waje dan kar Aliya taji karar motarsa 


Har ya shige b'angarensu Amir babu Wanda ya ganshi Luckily kuma k'ofar a bud'e yake har na palon

A tsaye ya hangota ta juyawa k'ofar shigowa b'aya tana d'auko Abu daga fridge d'in dake palon sanye take da wani pink d'in shimi mai k'aramin Hannu  iya gwiwa ga shatukan belt nan akwakwance ajikinta yayi ja 


Wani irin Yawu ya had'iye ya hard'e hannayensa a  kirjinsa yana kare mata kallo 


Zara kuwa haka kawai taji kamar ana kallonta ahankali ta juya ta ware idonta sosai tana kallon Adnan dake mata kallon Kurilla  


Adnan murmushi ya sakar mata 

Zara itama ta sakar mishi murmushin tana rufe fridge d'in tare da d'aukar robar yoghurt d'in data Ciro daga fridge d'in 

Daga bakin k'ofar Adnan yace "ya jikin naki"?

" am good Alhamdulillah naji sauki ya akayi ka dawo da wuri haka"?

Murmushi Adnan ya k'ara yi dake sa Zara k'ara narkewa a kogin sansa yace "am done with wat am doing shine nace bari na dawo and check on you ina office ne amma hankalina na wajenki"


Zara itama murmushin tayi a lokacin da take zama akan one seater dake kallon k'ofar tace "don't u think is risky yanda kake shigowa kanka tsaye indan mai gidan ya kamaka fa"?

" You are funny ya kamani kuma sai kace wani kwarto responsiblityn dake kansa daya kasa kula dashi nake taya shi kula  dashi"


Zara girgiza kanta tayi tace "hakane thanks ban shirya k'arbar wani bulalan ba u can go zan maka magana anjima ta watsapp but now am scared"


"Ashe haka kike da tsoro bansani anyway since u insist bari na tafin sai najiki do u care for anything let me get it for u"?

Wani irin farinciki ne ya rufe Zara dan haka takes an namiji mai balain caring murmushi tayi tace " no thanks"

Adnan watsa hannuwansa yayi tare da dage kafad'a yace "shikenan sai najiki"

Daga haka ya juya ya tafi Zara kuwa da sauri ta koma wajen window tana kallonsa Dan bil hakki ta bala'in kamuwa da San Adnan ahaka ta tsaya a window har ya shigo da motarsa ya fito ya nufi b'angarensa 


Sai da ya shige Zara ta saki labulen window taje ta zauna tana ta  kitsima abubuwa aranta 


Adnan kuwa yana shiga gida ya d'aura fuskarsa sosai irin no nonsense d'inan bai ga Aliya a palon ba bai kuma damu da ya gantan ba ya shige d'akinsa

Aliya kuwa taji motsin dawowarsa amma ta k'asa motsawa Dan wani irin sanyi taji yana shigarta kota ina so take ma ta nemi taimakonsa ya kaita koda chemist sabida azabar ciwon kan da take sha amma ina ko hannunta ta k'asa d'agawa 


Adnan kuwa kwanciyarsa yayi a d'akinsa yana hango surorin Zara a zuciyarsa yana ayyana abubuwa da dama aransa game da ita bai tab'a jin sha'awar mace sosai kamar yanda yake Jin sha'awar Zara abunka da shaidan gani yake idan bai samu ya kashe kishin daya taso masa ba zai iya mutuwa data ya kunna ya hau WhatsApp ko zai ga maganarta amma ina babu bakuwar lambar da ta masa magana in banda Meera data mishi dogon pages na cin mutunci da tsinuwa ko gama karantawa bai yi ba yayi clearing tare da blocking d'inta.


Sai da ya iddar da Sallar isha ya fito Dan yaci abinci Dan bai samu zuwa massallaci ba sabida tunanin banza Dana wofi da ya ringayi


Wayam yaga bai ga kulollin da Aliya ta saba jera mishi ba akan dining 

Take ransa ya b'aci wani Haushin Aliya ya k'ara ya rufeshi wato Dan iskanci abin nata ya kai har girki tak'i tayi masa 


A fusace ya nufi d'akinta yana kwalla mata Kira 

Aliya duk da jikin da takeji hakan bai hana tsoron Adnan ya rufeta ba sabida irin Kiran da yake mata 


Adnan a fusace ya janye bargon data lullub'a dashi yana shek'a masifa akan mai yasa bata mishi abinci ba uban mai takeso yaci ko 

Hucin jikin Aliya daya bugeshi Da
 jajjayen idonta da hawayen dake zubowa ta gefen fuskarta ne yasa ya tsayar da maganar da yake yana kare mata kallo 


Aliya kuwa ganinsa yasa wani kuka ya kwace mata 

"Ke kuma mai ya sameki haka"?


Shine Kalmar da yafito daga bakin Adnan yana d'an tab'e fuska 

Cikin cije bak'i da azabar da kanta ke yi ta gaya masa abinda yake damunta

Adnan fad'a ya rufeta dashi yana " ba dole kiyi ciwon kai da zazzabi ba kin bi kin saka masifa aranki Allah ya sauwake sai ki tashi muje chemist a duba ki"

Adnan yace yana k'arasa yaye mata bargon dake jikinta 

Shi ya taimaka mata ta mik'e yana ta shek'a mata masifa  


Sai da aka mata allura aka loda mata  uban magunguna sabida yanda zazzabin ya mata mugun kamu a hanya Adnan ya tsaya ya siya musu kaza da lemuna masu sanyi 


Aliya dak'yar ta iya cin Rabin Cinyar kaza sabida rashin appetite ta sha magungunanta ta nufi d'aki tayi sallolinta ta kwanta 

Adnan kuwa tunda yagama cin kazarsa ya koshi ya nad'e a dogon kujera yana ta jiran message d'in Zara ko ta kan Aliya bai k'ara bi ba 



Zara kuwa so take sai tazo kwanciya sai ta mishi magana ta WhatsApp 


Ameer kuwa tunda ya bar gida yafara Dana sanin dukan da yayi wa Zara bai tab'a tunanin zai iya d'aga hannu ya daki mace ba bai San meyesa Zara take mishi haka ba sabida San da yake mata take mishi abubuwa iri iri yana shanyewa amma abun nata yayi yawa idan ba rashin hankali ba ya za'ayi ta fito da sleeping dress ta tsaya agaban namijin da ba nata ba babu Wanda ya cancanci yaganta da wanan shigar sai shi wani zubin idan yana ganin rashin kamun kanta sai yaji ina ma ace zata yi koyi da Halin Matar Adnan zai iya cewa bai tab'a ganinta da mayafi ba tana iya k'ok'arinta wajen kare mutuncinta tasan darajar Aure sosai Dan ko da gaishe shi zatayi kanta a sunkuye shi bai ma San kamaninta ba sosai da Hijabinta dake Neman sharar k'asa yake ganewa itace he wish haka Zaran sa take amma Zara zai iya cewa Hijabinta d'aya na lefenta duk ta bayar dashi d'ayan ma shi ya siyo mata Dan ta ringa sawa 


With deep regret ya wuni ranar yasha alwashin bata hakuri da kuma yi mata nasih duk da akwai abubuwan da bai kammalla ba haka ya hakura karfe Tara ya nufi gida bayan kayan kwalam da makulashe daya loda Dan ya kai mata sabida sune cimarta.....*M🅰K'OT🅰N JUN🅰*

*page 5*



Ameer a Palo ya tarar da Zara tana shafawa tab'on jikinta mai daga ita sai towel Dan bata Dade da fitowa daga wanka ba 

Tunda taji karar motar Ameer ta d'aura fuskarta sosai babu alamar faraa a tare da ita 


Ameer sallama ya k'ara yi akaro na biyu still bata amsa ba dama yasan zaa rina ko da ba fad'a suka yi ba bata wani damu ta amsa Sallama ba 


Ledojin hannunsa ya ajiye yace cikin sanyin murya y "Sannu da gida"

Kamar yanda yayi tunanin bazata kula shin ba ko haka akayi Dan ko d'aga kanta batayi ta kalleshi ba ballantana ta kalli inda ya ke 


Kusa da ita ya zauna ya tankwashe k'afarsa ya ruk'o hannunta 

Wani irin fusge hannunta tayi tare da sakar masa harara tana "don't u dere touch me Ameer na tsaneka wlh namijin da bai San darajar Mace ba shi yake d'aga hannu ya daketa bazan iya zama da namijin da zai ringa jibgata ba dan haka ka bani takardata kawai na tafi gidanmu"

Tace tana k'ara watsa mishi mugun kallo 


Ameer murmushi yayi Dan ko kad'an baiji haushin maganganunta ba he deserve more dan this dan sai a yanzu yaga yanda ya farfasa mata jiki 

Cikin Sanyi murya yafara magana 

"Zara am sorry pls I know what I did was wrong bai kamata na dakeki ba Amma Zara kinsan idan raina ya b'aci bana iya controlling d'in kaina 

Zara kinsan halina ina da bala'in kishi   mai yasa zaki fita da sleeping dress har gidan mak'otanmu shi kuma babu kunya babu tsoron Allah ya tsaya yana kare miki kallo Zara dan Allah haka kika ga matarsa ta nayi meyesa bazaki yi koyi da halinta ba 

 wlh har 
yau bansan kamanin matarsa ba sabida yanda take lullub'e jikinta 

Zara idan bana sanki babu yanda za'ayi nayi kishinki islamically ma is not right kifita ahaka kina matsayin matar Aure karki manta ko ina na jikin mace Al-aura ce banda fuskarta tafin kafarta da tafin hannayenta am really hurt Dana ganki agaban Adnan atsaye ahaka 

Zara ban hanaki kiyi shigar da ranki keso ba wlh Zara ko tsirara ne ma kiyi matsawar dagani sai ke ni zaki yiwa adon nagani naji dadi kisamu lada 

  ina rok'onki kiji tsoron Allah ki kiyaye dokokin Aure da martab'arsa ki ringa tsare mutuncin kanki ki ringa tayani kishin kanki pls insha Allah zan miki duk abinda kikeso matsawar bai fi k'arfina ba ki ringa koyi da mak'ociyarki pls she is very decent ga kunya wlh ban tab'a ganin fuskarta ba kullum kanta a sunkuye kuma"



Shiru yayi sakamakon wani mugun kallo da Zara ta watsa mishi tace "OK kana nufin am not decent and banida kunya idan hakane mai zai hana kaje ka aureta ni ka sakeni inje in Auri Wanda ya dace dani dan gaskiya bazan b'oye maka ba wlh ina bala'in Dana sanin Aurenka ka takura min ka hanani sakewa ban tab'a ganin  ranar data fito ta fad'i batare da kayimin fad'a ba agidanan kullum cikin yimin fad'a kake ban  tab'a yin Abu nayi dai-dai ba 

Ameer katseta yayi da " ina miki fad'ane cos u are  not doing the Right thing Zara babu yanda za'ayi na hau yi miki fad'a kawai ba tare da dalili ba


 idan kikayi ba dai-dai ba ban nusar dake naganar dake ba mai amfanina amatsayina na mijinki ke kinfiso na zuba miki ido kawai ki ringa abinda ranki keso yanzu duba Sallar daya zama obligatory akanki  bakya San kiyi  sai kin ga dama 

A times  sai na tsaya akanki kikeyi wai Nan da sunan kina cikakkiyar musulma and ahaka zakiyi raising children d'inki suna ganin yanda kike yi Zara marriage is not all about sex and satisfaction u are a woman u have to be a role model to your children abinda kikeyi shi zasu yi koyi da shi amma zaki so ki haifi 'ya'ya suga  bakya salla sai kin ga dama  ga jin music na tashin hankali ga dressing any how haba Zara ki duba magana ta bawai ina takura miki bane wlh gaskiya nake fad'a miki nan gaba zaki gane hakan 

idan kika bi abubuwan danace wlh zamanmu zai yi dadi ni ba mai takurawa  bane"

Zara duk maganar dayake ta bayan kunnenta yake shiga yake fita da yake shaidan na mata hudubar Tsiya bala'in haushinsa take ji har wani tsanarsa take ji yana darsuwa a ranta d'agowa tayi tace


"Sheik Ameer kamar yanda na gaya maka gaskiya wlh daga yau bazan yarda ka k'ara takura min ba idan kaga dama ka yankani inaga kuma bazan iya ba nayi tafiyata gidanmu ka biyoni da takadata ai ni tunda naga take takenka nake shan pills d'in hana haihuwa dan bazan zo ka ringa samun pressure ba ina d'auke da ciki and beside am not even ready to be a mother haka kawai nan da nan na tsufa kayi abandoning d'ina"


"Zara yanzu kina nufin kina shan magani sabida karki d'au ciki"? 

Ameer yace ransa a d'an b'ace 

" kwarai kuwa dan ban shirya haihuwa ba yanzu"


Zara tace tana juyar dakai

Ameer zuba mata ido yayi zuciyarsa na masa zafi ya zakayi aure dan ka samu zuria da albarkar Aure amma ace kai kake shan magani dan kar ka samu ya lura ba iya iskanci ne yasa take haka ba har da jahilci da rashin ilimi da ace yasan haka halinta yake da babu abinda zai sa ya aureta dan Sam ta bakin nata basu dace da juna ba amma ya zai yi kaddara ta Riga fata 


Ameer ganin idan ya cigaba da kallonta ransa zai cigaba da b'aci ne yasa ya mik'e ya nufi d'akinsa


Zara kuwa ta rakashi da harara tana janyo ledar tare da fito da da kayayyakin da ya siyo Wanda duk cimarta ne 

Hankalinta kwance taci pizza dinta da kora da lemo ta maida sauran kayan cikin fridge ta nufi d'akinta batare da ta kalli D'akin Ameer ba burinta kawai ta hau WhatsApp tayi chtting da Adnan.



Sai da ta kwanta ta kunna data tayiwa Adnan magana da 

"Hi"

Adai-dai lokacin Adnan ya zubawa wayarsa ido ko yaya yaji shigowar message sai yayi sauri ya duba har ya fara fidda rai da zata mishi magana 

Yaga bakuwar lambar 

Ganin message d'in da aka turo mishi yasa yayi sauri ya mik'e wani irin farinciki na ratsa shi jiki na rawa yayi mata reply da hello 

Ya bud'e profile pic d'inta sanye take da wani long sleeve fari ta bak'in hula rabin kirjinta a waje dan Ameer shi bama chattn yake ba ballantana yasan irin hoton da take d'orawa 

Ai Adnan wani irin zillo yayi   daya k'ara zooming d'in hoton yana lashe lebb'e sai ayyana Abubuwa yake aransa atake yayi saving hoton 

B'angaren Zara itama hoton Adnan ta bud'e dake profile d'insa aikuwa sai data tashi zaune sabida yanda hoton yayi bala'in tafiya da imaninta dan ba k'aramin kyau Adnan yayi a hoton ba sanye yake da wani red d'in Riga yazo ya d'ora facing cap ga wani arnen glass dayasa a rayuwarta tana bala'in San namiji yayi shigar kananan kaya Ameer shi kuwa basu dame shi ba yafisa manyan kaya  itama saving d'in hoton tayi jiki na rawa tace 

"Sorry for d disturbance dafatan baka fara bacci ba"


Adnan da sauri ya mata reply da 

"Ya za'ayi princess tace na jira message d'inta nayi bacci ya kike ya jikin naki"?


Murmushi Zara tayi kamar yana kallonta tace 

" am OK thanks for today I really appreciate"


"Nah don't mention is my pleasure u are looking breathtaking and stunning I must admit u are really beautiful Zara gaskiya Ameer yayi dacen mata"

Zara pillo ta d'auko ta kankame a kirjinta sai murmushi take a duniya tanaso ace tayi kyau

"Uhmm tsokana ta kake kaima har na kai Queen d'inka kyau?

Adnan- " amma kin cuci kanki idan kika had'a kanki da wanan bakauyiyar matar ina ma regretting yanda akayi na aureta irinku na yawo agari


Ai Zara ji take kamar ta daka tsalle dan murna ta k'ara rungume pillonta a kirjinta dan tana bala'in jin dadin hirar da suke cewa tayi 

"Really"? 

" yea" 

Hira suka ringa yi sosai inda Adnan ya bukaci ta turo mishi hotunanta 

Aikuwa zafafan hotunanta guda goma ta tura mishi ko ajikinta dan  ita kam Ta fad'a kogin San Adnan ko aurenta ne zata iya kashewa indai yace zai Aureta

Ameer kuwa daya yi sallarsa da 'yan nafilolinsa ji yayi b'acin ran dayake ciki ya ragu kwanciya yayi akan sallaya yana tunanin yanda zai gyara gidansa su daina samun matsala da Zara ko a islamiyya zai yi enrolling d'inta kila idan ta d'an samu ilimi addini zata gyara kurakuranta ta San darajar Aure da bin miji sai da yasamu awa d'aya akwance yana tunane tunane inda a karshe ya tashi ya cire doguwar rigarsa ya kwanta amma me Sam ya k'asa bacci ji yayi yana murd'in matarsa ba tare da wani tunani ba ya mik'e idonsa akan agogo k'arfe d'aya da minti goma d'akin Zara ya nufa 

A lokacin Zara ta koma ta kwanta tana jin dadin yanayin da take ciki dan Adnan is romantic iya kalmomin daya mata kawai ya saukar mata da kasala babu abinda yake sai kurzanta kyaunta yana wishing dama shine Ameer 


Bata tab'a tunanin Ameer zai shigo d'akinta ba sai kawai gani tayi ya shigo 

Gabanta sai da ya fad'i amma a fuska bata nuna hakan ba dan d'aura fuska tayi tana typing da sauri da Sai gobe Ameer ya shigo daga haka ta kashe wayar gabad'aya ta ajiye a karkashin pillonta ta juyawa ameer baya dake zama a gefen gadon


Sau biyu ya kira sunanta amma ko amsawa bata yi ba

Murmushi yayi dan yasan hanyar da yake bi da ita nan da nan ta sauko daga dokin fushin data hau 

Gadon ya hau sosai ya kwanta abayanta yafara k'ok'arin kunna duk channels d'in dake  jikinta 

Tureshi tayi da k'arfi tare da zabga mishi harara Wanda hakan yayi bala'in bawa Ameer mamaki dan tunda sukayi aure kowane irin fad'a sukayi matsawar zai kusanceta sun shirya kenan bata tab'a bijire mishi ba inya nemeta daga haka suke shiryawa idan sunyi fad'a 


Da d'an mamaki ya k'ara rungumota ta kuwa k'ara tureshi da iya k'arfinta dan bala'in haushinsa takeji ita fuskar ma Adnan ke mata yawo a ido Shaidan kuwa ya cigaba da buga mata ganga

Da mamaki Ameer yace "Zara lafiya kuwa mai haka"?

" kamarya fa ko ana dolene ka gama jibgata kawani kwaso jiki ka taho zaki hayeni bazai yiwu ba  wlh tunda ka dakeni nida kai ne a gidanan"


Ameer da mamaki ya kashe a zaune cewa yayi "amma naga baki hakuri yanzu kina so kice bazaki bani hakkina ba"

"Au kasan Kanada hakki awajena ka jibgeni dazu to inda kake so kaje kayi mishi rauni dazu afarfashe yake sai ka hakura sai ya warke"


Ameer kallonta kawai yake yana bala'in mamakin sabon salon data tsiro mishi dashi janyota yayi da k'arfi ya cigaba da smushing d'inta aikuwa ta gartsa masa wani mugun cizo a baki ya saketa da sauri 

Harararsa tayi tana "haka kawai zaka zo kayimin fyade ko ana  dolene"?


Ameer duk yanda yakai yayi controlling b'acin ransa kasawa yayi sai da ya jawota ya murd'e mata hannu ya haye ruwan cikinta ya biya bukatarsa da ita sai kuka take tana kai masa duka da Allah ya isa 


Shi kuwa mik'ewa yayi still ransa a b'ace yabar d'akin yana tunanin hanyar da zai biyo mata dashi dan abun nata is out of hand



Adnan kuwa daya ga message d'in Zara sai da ransa ya b'aci wani kishi ya rufe shi dan gani yake Ameer yana can yana tab'a Zara ga wani tsananin sha'awa data taso masa ganin idan bai samu ya biya bukatarsa ba za'a samu Matsala yasa ya nufi d'akin Aliya 


Bai dubi halin da take ciki ba ya afka mata 

Aliya kuwa da azabar ciwo ta kalleshi tana mishi magiyar ya hakura zuwa gobe bazata iya ba amma ina ai kamar zuga Adnan tayi dan sai daya gurjeta sosai dan for sure he enjoy being with her ba karya Allah yayiwa matarsa niima jarraba ce kawai irin ta d'a namiji 


Aliya kuwa numfashin wahala kawai ta ringa fitarwa dan ko a lokacin da take da lafiya Adnan bai mata irin wanan muguntar ba adaddafe ta Shiga band'aki ta tsarkaka jikinta ta dawo ta kwanta a lokacin har bacci yayi awon gaba da Adnan.

*page 6*


Aliya zuba mishi ido tayi tana kallon shi kaunar mijinta na ratsa ta ahankali tasa hannunta tana shafa fuskarsa Allah yasan bata da burin da ya wuce taga ta farantawa mijinta ko kad'an bata so taga tana b'ata mishi rai bata San mai yake damunsa ba yabi ya canja mata tayi iya k'ok'arinta dan ta gano abinda take mishi yabi ya canja mata haka ta k'asa abun ya kai ya kawo har dasu zagi 



Tunane tunane Aliya ta ringa yi na yanda zata kuma dawo da zaman lafiya da farinciki gidanta inda a karshe tayi yanke shawarar da zata bi ta kuma gamsu da shawarar daga haka ta matsa kusa dashi ta rungume shi tsam dan har ta daina jin zazzabin sai dai rashin kwarin jiki.



Ameer kuwa aranar kwanan k'unci da bak'in ciki yayi tabbas Zara tayi nisa  bata jin kira dole ya d'au mataki akanta dan ba a bariki ya Aurota ba da zata ringa mishi abinda ranta keso ahaka ya kwana yana tunanin hanyar da zai bi shima ya gyara gidansa.


Washegari da sassafe Aliya ta tashi bayan ta idar da sallah ta fad'a kitchen ta had'a musu breakfast ta fito taci uban kwalliya da wani shegen doguwar Riga da ya bayyana kowane sura na jikinta gaban rigar da bayan rigar  duk a tsage har wajen cinya  bama ta tab'a sa kayan ba ita kanta k'asa d'auke idonta tayi daga mudubin sabida yanda tayi balain kyau  tasan yau sai ta burge Adnan 

Gashinta da bai da tsayi sosai  sai cika tayi  combing tare oling ta kame shi da ribon mai dan girma tayi balain kyau kuwa sai zabga murmushi take dan tasan yau 
 Adnan zai yaba mata dan  hatta girkin data yi yau favourite d'insa ne 

Sai data had'a mishi ruwan wanka ta nufi wajen sa dan ta tashe shi  yayi wanka 

Adnan kuwa tunda yayi sallah ya bararaje yana mafarki da Zara dan ya sata aransa sosai yana cikin  mafarki  yaje dubata tace tazo su shiga daga 
 d'aki suna  shiga ta janyo shi ta rungume shi  Aliya ta fara tashinsa


Wani irin takaici ne ya hana ya bud'e idonsa ita kuwa Aliya ta zauna agefensa ta fara shafa mishi kai tana wasa da yatsun hannunsa 

Kamshin turaren Aliya ne yasa ya bud'e idonsa aikuwa ya waresu sosai yana kallonta dan ba karya ta masifar yi mishi kyau 


Aliya kuwa murmushi ta sakar masa tana k'ara shafa Kansa cikin sanyin murya tace "Dear ka tashi kayi wanka kada ka makara"

Adnan da babu inda yake kallo sai kirjinta dan bata sa bra ba aikuwa janyota yayi kamar wani tsohon mayye yafara lasheta dan ta tayar masa da hankali sosai 

Aliya itama martani ta ringa mayar masa sosai inda suka farantawa juna rai sosai ta kuma ji dadin yanda Adnan ya yaba da adon da tayi dan tasan shi ya taimaka wajen rikita shi 


Bayan sun gama wrestling Aliya na kwance a jikinsa Adnan shi kuma ya lumshe idonsa yana shafa mata kai 

Aliya  Sunansa ta kira  Adnan ya bud'e idonsa ya na kallonta 

"Dear bana so muringa samun sabani dakai a rayuwata bani da burin da ya wuce na faranta maka rai pls idan ina maka Abu ba dai-dai ba  batare da nasani ba dan Allah ka gayamin na gyara banajin da din yanda ka canjamin   baka nunawa ina gabanka pls ka fad'amin abinda nake ma in gyara"

Adnan shiru yayi na wajen minti biyar dan Sam bai San abinda zai ce mata ba  babu abinda zai iya cewa ta masa da yake mata hakan jarraba ce kawai irin nashi


K'ara rungumeta yayi a jikinsa yace 

"Babu abinda kika min Aliya karki damu abubuwa ne suka sha min kai amma zan gyara ki kwantar da hankalinki babu abinda kika min"


Aliya d'agowa tayi tace "are u sure"?

" yea am sure ki kwantar da hankalinki"


Tare sukayi wanka ta taimaka mishi wajen shiryawa suka fito suka karya Rabon da Aliya ta tsinci kanta acikin farinciki haka har ta manta dan Adnan ya saki jiki da ita sosai sun soye

Adnan shi ma wasai yaji zuciyarsa dan duk da yana Neman mata har yanzu bai samu wacce ta kai matarsa taste ba faranta masa da Aliya tayi ya taimaka  shi wajen rage tunanin Zara da abinda yake ji game da ita na azazzaban sha'awa 


Har mota Aliya ta Raka shi bayan ta saka dogon hijabinta dake sharar k'asa dan Riga da wando tasa bayan ta k'ara wanka 



Zara da tafi Minti ashirin a tsaye ajikin window dan tasan yana fita da wuri mafarki tayi da Adnan sosai Wanda bata so ma ta farka ba ba dan Ameer ya tasheta ba dan tayi sallah kuma tunda ya tasheta ta k'asa komawa bacci sai tunanin Adnan take tana kallon hotonsa shaidan yana k'ara rura mata wutar so da sha'awar Adnan 

Hango Adnan da tayi da Aliya kafad'a na gugan kafad'a ne yasa gabanta ya fad'i take hankalinta ya tashe dan Adnan murmushi kawai yake yayi wani fresh dashi 

Zara jikinta ne ya fara rawa a lokacin da Aliya tayi d'age tayiwa Adnan kiss a kumatu shi kuwa Adnan kamar cewa akayi ya kalli b'angaren su Zara ya kuwa hango ta ta zuba mishi ido 

K'asa d'auke kansa yayi shima ya zuba mata ido dan sleeping dress ne ajikinta mai hannun shimi Rabin kirjinta a waje


Aliya dake ta masa fatan dawowa lafiya ganin ya kurawa waje d'aya ido yasa itama ta juya dan taga mai yake kallo aikuwa Zara tayi sauri ta durk'usa 

Adnan kuwa yayi sauri ya d'auke kansa yana shigewa mota tare da sakarwa Aliya murmushi yace mata sai ya dawo 


Sai da yabar harabar gidan ta juya dan ta koma ciki sai a lokacin ta tuna Marin da tayiwa Zara kuma ko kad'an bata yi nadamar abinda tayi ba dan idan ba haka tayi mata ba bazata shiga hankalinta ba haka za tayi ta rashin hankalin ta na zuwa b'angarenta tsirara .



Zara kuwa ji tayi kanta na Sara mata sabida tsananin kishin da ya rufeta a dogon kujerar palon ta kwanta ta runtse idonta tana  hango yanda Aliya tayiwa  Adnan kiss yana murmushi wato yana San matarsa duk da 'yar kauyen da yake ce mata yana regretting Aurenta 


Tsayuwar Ameer akanta ne yasa ta bud'e idonta tana kallonsa 


Fuska a d'an d'aure Ameer yace "ki tashi ki dafa min indomie da kwai zan fita aiki"


Wani kallon Tara saura kwata Zara ta mishi tace "bazan iya ba kaina ciwo yake"

"Bazaki iya ba fa kikace"

Ameer yace ransa na kuma b'aci 


"Eee bazan iya ba gaskiya inji da dukan ma kawai da kamin jiya"

Ameer zama yayi akan kujerar dan yaga  bin Zara da yake yi yasa take rashin mutuncin da ranta keso 

"Zara kinsan dai ba Auren bariki mukayi dake ba da sai abinda kika ga dama zaki ringa yi ban isa na Saka ki Abu ba kiyi   acikin gidanan 

Aure ana yinsa ne dan asamu kwanciyar hankali da farinciki mai d'orewa har sai ta Allah ya kasance amma tunda naga alamar kina da k'arancin ilimin addini bakisan komai game da zaman aure ba ballantana akai da kisan dokokinsa zan maidaki gida mahaifiyarki ta koya miki idan kin iya zaman Auren na dawo dake dan babu bambancina da Wanda ba shi da Aure tunda abinci ma bazaki iya girka min ba dan haka ki tashi mu tafi na maidaki gidanku"


Zara mamakin maganganun Ameer ne ya rufeta amma ko a fuska bata nuna mishi ba illa ma mik'ewa da tayi tana "nama sha iska wlh dan gaskiya ka takura min kuma zaman hakurin nima nakeyi dakai at least zan d'an samu freedom"


Daga haka ta nufi d'akinta da Sauri dan ta saka kayanta dan gani take da Wanan hanyar zata samu tayi abinda ranta keso 


Ameer kuwa ko ajikinsa shima ya nufi d'aki dan ya saka kayansa dan ya gaji da irin Abubuwan da take mishi gwara ya kaita gidansu ko zasu mata fad'a ta gyara halinta tunda duk abinda take dama bai tab'a kai kararta ba.


K'arfe goma da rabi  Ameer yayi parking a k'ofar gidansu Zara 

Zara kuwa tayi sauri tafito tana janye akwatun da ta lodo kayanta aciki

Shigewarta tayi tabar Ameer a tsaye 

Ameer kuwa ganin shiru shiru babu alamar za'ayi mishi iso ya shiga gidan yasa ya tura yaro da ayi mishi sallama da Dr Kabir mahaifin Zara kasancewarsa likita yasa ake ce mishi doctor


Yaron dawowa yayi yace wai ya shiga

Da yake yasan kan gidan direct d'akin saukar baki ya nufa dake gefen gidan ya shiga ya zauna sai da ya kwashi minti talatin a zaune har yana shirin mik'ewa ya tafi sai gashi ya shigo da katon cikinsa fuskarnan tasa a d'aure dan har Zara ta shimfid'a masa karya da gaskiya ya hau kai ya zauna 


A dak'ile ya amsa gaisuwar da Ameer ya mishi 


Ameer shi kuwa bai tsaya b'ata lokaci ba ya zayyana masa irin abubuwan da Zara ke mishi da kuma dalilin dayasa ya dawo da ita gida ta koyi wasu abubuwan kafin ya maidata 



Dr Kabir kuwa farin glass d'in fuskarsa ya gyara fuska a murtuk'e yace "a duk maganar nan da kamin baka fad'amin abinda tayi kayi mata irin Wanan dukar ba ka faffasa mata jiki Wanda ni mahaifinta tunda na haifeta ban tab'a mata irin shi ba"

Ameer shiru yayi dan bai San abinda zai ce mishi ba dan aganinsa bayanai daya mishi ya isa yasa yagane abinda ya tunzura shi ya daketa 

A d'an tsawace Dr Kabir ya k'ara tambayar Ameer yana "nace ka fad'amin dalilin dayasa ka Dakar min 'ya har sai da ta suma Wanan wane irin ranshi hankaline angaya maka jaka ka Aura ka mata mugun dukan baka kaita hospital an duba rauni daka mata ba amma sabida rashin hankali ka kuma dawo da ita gabana kana so kace min bata da tarbiyya there is no man born of a woman dat would treat my daughter d way he likes cos yarana da gatansu ka gane ko ka san baka Santa mai yasa ka aureta pls ka tashi ka fita nima sai na nemeka"

Ameer da sauri ya mik'e sabida kar zuciya ta d'eb'eshi yayi ba dai dai ba dan ba k'aramin tafasa zuciyarsa ke yi ba sai a yanzu ya gano inda Zara ta d'auko rashin tarbiyarta dan dattijon arziki bazai yi abinda Dr Kabir yayi ba yayi balain dana sanin Auren Zara a fusace yaja motar yabar unguwar.



B'angaren Adnan kuwa tunda ya zauna a office Zara ke mishi gizo dan tunda yaganta dazu yaji kuma babu wacce yakesan kasancewa da ita sama da ita yana tsoron ya kirata Ameer na nan juyi kawai yake akan kujerar dayake zaune duk da moment d'insa da Aliya na kwakwalwarsa ta kuma kirashi yanzu ya kai sau uku dan kawai taji ya yaje office banda texes da ta mishi na soyayya da fatan alheri sure Aliya na iya k'ok'arinta dan ta nuna kulawarta akansa amma ina duk wanan fad'i tashin da take yi baya gani

Watssap ya hau yaga ko tana kai yaga rabonta da hawa tun jiya daddare ahankali ya maida wayarsa ya ajiye ko mai ya tuna ya d'auki wayar sa da Sauri ya shiga Zgallery ya bud'o hotunanta ya na kallo aikuwa numfashinsa ya hau fita da sauri dan sai yanzu yayiwa hotunan kallon tsanake ta had'une iya had'uwa abunka da shaidan tuni ya hau buga mishi ganga Adnan kuwa yabi ya susuce yaga idan ba ganin Zara yayi ba hankalinsa ba zai tab'a kwanciya ba 

Dak'yar ya bari k'arfe d'aya tayi ya latso numberta 

Adai dai lokacin da Zara itama tayi dailing number sa dan ta kira shi tace mishi tana gida ganin kiransa ya shigo yasa tayi sauri ta d'aga wayar wani farinciki na ratsata 

A tare suka lumshe ido 

Zara ta koma ta kwanta tana hugging pillo shi kuma Adnan cikin muryarsa dake rikita mace yace "dafatan dai ban kira oga na nan ba"

Tsaki Zara tayi tace "wane oga kuma ni danake gidanmu"

Zaro ido Adnan  yayi kamar yana gabanta yace "bangane kina gida ba ba dazu na ganki ba"?

" uhmm dazu naganka  kana zabga soyayya da matarka"

Zara tace tana hararar wayar kamar yana kallonta 

Murmushi Adnan yayi yace "dan Allah joke apart dagaske kina gidanku mai ya faru haka"?

" mai ma bai faru ba gani nayi bazan iya cigaba da zama yana jibgata haka kawai sai kace baiwa ya samu shiyasa nayi dawowata gida"


Adnan ji yayi kamar ya daka tsalle ya taka rawa amma kuma a fili yace "kai amma banji dadi ba yanzu haka zai zauna shi kadai babu mace"

Zara wani hararar ta k'ara dokawa wayar dan so tayi ya nuna farinciknsa amma a fili tace"dama can shi kad'ai yake rayuwarsa ni yanzu ma ji nayi kamar anyi freeing d'ina ayi mutum babu abinda yasani sai takura da masifa"


Adnan dake murmushi da wani shegen murya yace "yanzu ya zakiyi kema haka zaki zauna a gidanku ba mai d'eb'e miki kewa"?

Zara da haushi ya gama cikata dan ba abinda take expecting yace kenan ba cewa tayi " babu komai friends d'ina zasu na d'eb'e min kewa"

Adnan k'ara k'asa yayi da muryarsa yanda zai kashe mata jiki yace "if am permitted zan iya zuwa na d'eb'e miki kewa"

Without second taught Zara tace he is permitted ta bashi address d'in gidansu dan k'arfin hali irin na Adnan har da tambayarta abinda zai taho mata dashi ita kuwa ta lissafa mishi yace mata sai yazo yayi sauri ya katse wayar dan tunda ya fara wayar Aliya ke kiranshi 


Aliya da jikinta ya gama sanyi da irin wayar da Adnan yake yi dan babu yanda za'ayi ace da namiji yake waya haka k'ila da mace yake waya tana jin ya d'aga murya asanyaye tace "inata kiran wayarka kana busy"

D'an had'e rai Adnan yayi dan gani yake Aliya ta fara takura mishi cewa yayi "eee wlh da manager nake waya kinsan yana Lagos ya akayine"

Aliya jinsa kawai tayi bata yarda dashi ba amma bata San ta b'ata mishi rai cewa tayi "ba komai dama cewa nayi ko zaka turo a d'aukar maka abincin rana ba sai kayi wahalar siya ba"

Adnan dake jinsa a koshe sabida maganar da sukayi da Zara cewa yayi "haba ba sai kin wahalar da kanki ba bana jin yunwa ki bari kawai idan na dawo naci na dare kuma yau bazan dawo da wuri ba zan d'an biya wani wajen karki jirani kici abincinki kiyi baccinki kawai"

Aliya gabanta ne yad'an fad'i tana tunanin kila wajen buduruwa zashi d'aurewa tayi tace "Allah ya dawo dakai lafiya love you"

Adnan bai mata reply ba ya kashe wayarsa 

Aliya kuwa tabi wayar da kallo tana mamakin Halin Adnan ko ba'a fad'a mata ba Akwai macen dake d'auke masa hankali awaje kuma zata sa ido ta binciko koma wacce.....*M🅰K'OT🅰N JUN🅰*

Mahaifiyar Zara Mommy Hauwa tunda Zara ta zo gidan taji dalilin da yasa ta dawo gida bak'in ciki da takaici suka rufeta Wanda har sai da ya kaita ga zubar da hawaye shekara 32 kenan tana k'unsar  bak'in cikin Auren Doctor Mahaifin Zara ba shi da Hali Sam Sam dan  rayuwa kawai yake ba tsari ya d'auki dab'iar turawa ya ara ya yafa bai da cikakken addini Sam sai kace daga kansa aka fara zama likita dan idan ba mantawa tayi ba har suka shekara goma da Aure yana zuwa party da neme neman 'yan mata har gida yake kawosu tayi musu girki yayi ta ce mata bagidajiya dan Auren Had'i aka musu shi ba ita yaso ya aura ba wata cos mate d'insa bayarabiya yaso ya aura  mahaifinsa ne yayi mishi fin k'arfi ya aura mishi ita duk da buduruwar tasa Ola tayi Aure basu fasa soyayya da doctor ba .

 a cikin   'ya'yansu guda biyar da suka Haifa d'ayane kawai ya fita Zakka Khaleed amma Aysha first born d'inta Abdulsalam, Zara,sadiq dukansu sun samu gurb'acewar tarbiyya daga mahaifinsu dan babu dama suyi ba dai dai ba ta hanasu bata mantawa yasha kwad'a mata mari idan zata hukuntasu sabida suyi sallah sai yace yarane ta daina takura musu.

 duk abinda suka ga dama suke yi babu zagi ba k'wab'a sanadiyar hawan jininta kenan dan ranaku d'adayane mazan basu jawo mata abun fad'a ba.

 Khalid ne kawai babu ruwan sa Aysha 'yayarsu kuwa duk da ta shekara goma da Aure harda 'ya'ya har gobe mijinta na kuka da k'arancin tarbiyyarta dan har kishiya yayi mata amma a banza bata fasa abinda take ba.

Gashi itama kanwarta ta d'auko irin halinta ba dama ta shiga cikin kawayenta ko cikin 'yan uwa sai an mata gorin rashin tarbiyyar 'ya'yanta 

Hawayen dake ta tsare a kumatunta ta goge dan kila ita nata jarrabawar kenan a gidan miji mik'ewa tayi ta nufi d'akin Zara.




Zara kuwa da yake tasan Halin Mahaifiyarta tunda ta gama shirga karya da gaskiya ta koma d'aki tak'i fitowa su had'u dan tasan fad'a Zata yi mata idan ba'ayi wasa bama tace zata mayar da ita ayau ita kuwa bata shiryawa hakan ba dan gaskiya ta samu opportunity da zata hole ta wataya San ranta.


Tana gama waya da Adnan bacci mai dadin gaske yayi awon gaba da ita.


Kamar a mafarki taji ana kiran sunanta tana bud'e ido taga mahaifiyarta a tsaye idonan nata a kumbure 


Waje ta samu ta zauna a gefen gadon tace ta tashi ta zauna magana zasuyi 


Zara sai data gama  k'unk'uni da zumbure zumburen baki ta tashi daga kwancen da take ta jawo pillo ta rungume a kirjinta tana kallan gefe 


Bata damu da yanda tayi ba dan sun Riga da sun saba basu da respect ko kad'an yanzu ma zasu nemi su zageka 


Magana ta farayi da muryarta da bata fita sosai sabida kukan da ta sha 

"Zara yanzu ke a ganinki hanyar da kika daukarwa kanki hanyace mai b'illewa"? 

Zara ina tausaya muku ranar da zakuyi nadamar hanyar da mahaifinku ya d'oraku akai tun kuna kananan yara nake nusar daku dan kugane mahaifinku ba gata ya ke muku ba wanan ba rayuwa bace halaka ce tsantsa Allah ya rufa miki asiri ya miki sutura ki yaye suturar da ya miki karki  ga yau ina zaune ina miki nasiha gobe bai zama lailai nayi miki ba indan na amsa kiran mahallici.

" Zara babu baban rufin asirin da ya wuce gidan aurenki na rok'eki da girman Allah ki koma d'akinki kiyiwa mijinki biyayya ki bishi sau da k'afa domin kisamu aljanarki Zara ki daina biyewa rud'in shedan yana kad'a miki ganga babu komai dake cikin duniyar nan face kyalkyala banza rayuwar 'ya mace gajeriya ce kiyi amfani da sauran lokacin da Allah ya ara miki kafin ya subuce miki ki shirya anjima na maidaki gidan mijinki ganinki agidanan k'ara tayar min da Hankali yake naji da abinda sauran kawai sukeyi"



Ta k'arasa zancen hawaye na kwararo mata

Duk maganar da takeyi Zara ji tayi kamar ihu take mata a kunne juyowa tayi tace 

"Mommy na koma fa kikace Kinsan irin dukan da yamin jiya kuwa kinsan irin zaman matsi da takura danake yi a gidansa kuwa ahaka nayi hakuri banyi magana ba da kansa kuma ya dawo dani gida sabida asara kuma sai na kwashi k'afafuna na koma kamar shi kad'ai ne namiji wlh bazan koma ba ko zan koma ba yanzu bai sai ya gane kurensa"


"A takaice kinaso ki nuna min ban isa dake ba bakuma na isa na baki shawara ba ko Zara"?

" Mommy nifa bance ba kawai banaso ki takurani akan sai na koma dan shi ya dawo dani kibari yanda ya dawo dani gida ya kuma dawowa ya maidani"


"To shikenan Zara karki koma ki cigaba da zama kina abunda ranki keso wataran idan aka baki kudi akace kiyi abinda ran naki yakeso baki isa kiyi ba"


Daga haka ta mik'e dan ta fice daga d'akin aikuwa kunnuwanta suka jiyo mata dogon tsakin da Zara taja da magana k'asa k'asa haka kawai abi a takurawa mutum"


Girgiza kanta tayi tafice daga d'akin da ahaka ma Zara tana d'an girmama ta akan sauran.


Tun k'arfe shidda  Zara ta fara shek'a kwalliya dama babu batun wani sallah sai anga dama akeyi kai idan kaga irin kwalliyar da tayi tunani zaka yi ko mijinta na sunna takewa sai da ta share sama da minti 30 tana nazarin kayan da zata sa inda a k'arshe ta fito da wani doguwar rigar material da ba'ayi mishi landing ba ta saka dogon wando daga ciki ta saka iya breziya idan ta tsaya babu abinda bazaka hango daga jikinta  ba.

 turarruka ta fara feswa a kowane lungu da sak'o na jikinta sai da  turaren ya fara hawa mata kai ta hakura da fesawa sai murmushi take ita kad'ai tana tunanin yanda zata burge Adnan 


Adnan kuwa sai da akayi Magriba ya nufi wani k'aton store ya ciko ledoji biyu da kayan kwalam da makulashe.

Musamman ya k'ara wanka a office hardasu brush dan kawai ya burge Zara 

A hankali yake tuk'a motarsa sanyi Ac Dana garin yasa yake jin wani nishadi  ga wani cool music dake tashi a motar



7:30 dai dai ya k'araso k'ofar gidansu Zara ya kirata a waya 

Zara dake k'ok'arin kiransa dan ta matsu ta sa shi a idonta sabida yanda shaidan ke rura mata wutan san shi a zuciyarta  


Da sauri ta d'aga wayar Adnan yace mata gashi awaje 

Sai data k'ara shafa hod'a a fuskarta ta gamsu da kyaun da tayi ta d'auko wani siririn ma yafi ta yafa duk da ta yafa mayafi kana hango farin breziyar data saka da Rabin nonuwanta dake waje.


Takun takalminta ne yasa Doctor, mommy Hauwa, Khalid,da sadiq dake zaune a Palo suna kallo  suka juya suna kallonta  


Doctor murmushi ya saki shi da Sadiq Mommy da Khalid kuwa suka saki baki suna kallon ikon Allah 

Zara kuwa bata damu da kallon da suke mata ba taje ta tsaya agefen kujerar da Doctor yake zaune tace "Daddy bari na d'an lek'a waje na dawo"

"OK dear take care of yourself"

Doctor yace yana shafa mata kai dan yana balain Santa a cikin 'yay'ansa 

Juyawa tayi da sauri ta nufi k'ofar fita daga palon da wani irin kallo Mommy Hauwa ke jifarta dashi.

Tsawan da ta daka mata ne yasa ta tsaya cak.

"Ke gidan ubanwa zakije daddaren nan da irin Wanan shigar a jikinki?kinada hankali kuwa? Ko kin manta kinada Aure?

" mommy nan waje zan fita fa yanzu nan zan dawo"


Zara tace tana zumb'uro mata baki tare da k'unk'uni 

Mommy Hauwa mik'ewa tayi a fusace dan ranta ya bala'in b'aci Zara ko da bata da Aure bai kamata ta saka rigar dake jikinta ba dan bak'in dogon wandon da breziyar dake jikinta duk ana hango shi 

Wani wawan Mari ta kwashe Zara dashi tana "Babu inda zakije da wanan shigar a jikinki idan ke jahila ce bakida ilimi ni ba jahila bace nasan dokokin Aure babu ma yanda za'ayi kifita daga gidanan ba tare da izinin mijinki dan haka maza wuce d'akinki kafin naci ubanki"

"Ke d'in banza ke d'in Wofi da zaki hana tafita ai ke sai dai naci ubanki awane dalili zaki bi ki takura mata ba sau d'aya ba ba sau biyu ba nasha fad'a miki ki daina takura wa 'ya'yana mijinta da bashi da hankali ya dukar min 'ya zakice Sai ta nemi izininsa to bazata nema ba yanda ba'a takura miki ba baki isa ki takura mata ba ke Zara wuce ki tafi"

Doctor yace shima yana huci

Cikin k'araji Mommy Hauwa ta fashe da kuka tana "Kabiru kabiru ka sani yanda kake da hakki akan yaranan haka nake da hakki akansu wanan ba kauna bace kasan matsayinta kuwa matar Aurece fa amma kace ta fita ahaka"

Tsaki yayi yace "get out my friend illiterate" ya wuce d'akinsa 

Sadiq shima mik'ewa yayi yace "wlh mommy yawa kike bakya ganewa mai dan Aunty Zara ta fita ahaka gaskiya kina da matsala ke bakisan yanzu haka shine show ba.

Ya nufi d'akinsa yana wani bouncing irin niggan nan

Mommy Hauwa ba a lokacin Sadiq yafara gaya mata magangnun  haka ba  tamkar ba ita ta tsugunna ta haifeshi ba amma me na yau d'in ya mata ciwo dan kaf cikin 'ya'yanta yafi kowa iskanci 

Kukan takaici ta fashe dashi tana zubewa akan kujera 

Khaleed ya tafi da gudu yana share mata hawaye yana bata hakuri shima yana hawayen.

Zara kuwa dama Doctor nacewa ta tafi tayi sauri ta fice dan ko zafin Marin bata ji ba sabida yanda take nishadin ganin Adnan 


Tundaga nesa Adnan ya fara kare mata kallo yana had'iyar yawu wani irin Abu na tsirga mishi har aransa abunka da shaidan gani yake Zara ta fita daban da sauran matan daya saba gani 


Zara cike da yanga ta k'ara sa cikin motar ta bud'e ta shige 

Wani wawan ajiyar zuciya Adnan ya sauke tare da lumshe ido sabida yanda kamshin turarenta ya doke hancinsa 

" Hi"

Zara tace da mayalwacin murmushi a fuskarta 

Adnan k'asa amsawa yayi in banda Wani mugun kwarton kallon dayake binta dashi nan da nan idonsa ya kankance zillo kawai manhood nashi nayi 

Zara k'ara gyara zamanta tayi tana fuskantar shi tasa hannunta ta d'anyi waving d'insa tana "hello yadai"

Ajiyar zuciya Adnan ya k'ara saukewa yana kallon nonuwanta dake shek'a mishi hoto yace can k'asan mak'oshi
